Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any nuclear_physics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 933. The projective resolution has length at most 532 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the nuclear_physics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^933 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 532-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
